```
2x2 grid, do this for 4 famous movies: Anchor: A 3D diorama of the actual film director (semantically inferred from the input film) intensely storyboarding at a director’s desk; the storyboard frames on the desk match the film’s signature composition, color language, and shot grammar; four floating cinematic “frame panels” show the film’s most iconic signature moments as storyboard-style grayscale frames with camera notes; behind them a deluxe “Director Edition” display box with the film title and director credit, featuring the main characters as dynamic collectible poses matching the film’s tone; floating props: clapboard, 35mm film strips, viewfinder, lens caps, marked script pages, clean studio sweep background::3.8
Morphology: premium stylized collectible figure with crisp hard-surface edges, camera-gear geometry, film-frame borders, storyboard annotation glyphs, motion arrows and blocking marks consistent with the film’s genre and era::3.2
Material Physics: glossy figure surface with subtle mold lines, matte paper storyboard cards with visible grain, semi-matte box with spot UV + foil title, translucent plastic window insert, realistic film strip sheen, crisp print registration, dust-free studio finish::3.2
Illumination: high-end studio product lighting, soft key + gentle fill, subtle rim light, controlled reflections, even exposure for legible storyboard notes and title text::1.6
Render Stack: collectible figure photography, shallow depth of field but readable text, isometric-leaning three-quarter angle, clean cyclorama background, sharp focus on face + hands + storyboard page, premium box art aesthetic::1.2
Negative: photoreal human skin, realistic pores, painterly texture, messy stains, low detail hands, illegible text, gibberish typography, watermarks, brand logos, extra limbs, deformed fingers, blurry frames, dull colors, harsh contrast:: -1.2
```

```
2x2 网格，为四部著名电影分别创作以下内容：锚点画面为一位3D微缩场景，展现该影片导演（根据输入片名语义推断）正专注地在导演桌前绘制分镜脚本；桌上的分镜画面精准还原该电影标志性的构图、色彩语言与镜头语法；四个悬浮的“电影画框面板”以分镜风格的灰度画面呈现该片最具代表性的经典瞬间，并附有摄影机位注释；背景中设有一款豪华“导演版”展示盒，印有电影标题与导演署名，盒面人物以契合影片氛围的动态可动人偶姿势呈现；悬浮道具包括场记板、35毫米胶片条、取景器、镜头盖、标注过的剧本页；背景为干净整洁的摄影棚布景。

造型风格：高端风格化可动人偶，具有清晰硬朗的表面边缘、摄影器材几何元素、胶片画幅边框、分镜注释符号，以及符合影片类型与年代特征的动作箭头与走位标记。

材质物理效果：人偶表面光亮并带有细微合模线，分镜卡纸为哑光且可见纸张纹理，展示盒半哑光并采用局部UV与烫金标题，透明塑料视窗插件，胶片条具有真实光泽感，印刷套色精准，整体呈现无尘摄影棚质感。

灯光：高端产品摄影棚灯光，柔和主光搭配轻柔补光，辅以微妙轮廓光，反射控制得当，曝光均匀以确保分镜注释与标题文字清晰可读。

渲染风格：可动人偶产品摄影，浅景深但文字清晰可辨，略带等距视角的三分之二角度构图，纯色无缝背景，面部、手部及分镜页面保持锐利焦点，整体呈现高端收藏盒艺术美学。

负面提示：避免写实人类皮肤、毛孔细节、绘画笔触质感、污渍杂乱、手部细节不足、文字不可读、无意义字体、水印、品牌标识、多余肢体、手指畸形、画面模糊、色彩沉闷、高对比度。
```

```
Natural Wonder of the Country Dioramas

Gemini Nano Banana Pro Prompt:

“Create an isometric miniature 3D diorama highlighting the natural landmark [NATURAL WONDER] from [COUNTRY NAME/PLANET].

Use a 40–45° top-down view. Textures look organic and realistic with PBR materials. Lighting feels soft and atmospheric.

The raised base shows terrain layers, vegetation, and surrounding geography. Add tiny stylized explorers or wildlife with no facial details.

Background remains solid [BACKGROUND COLOR].

Top center text displays [COUNTRY NAME] in bold.
Second line shows [NATURAL WONDER]. Place a minimal nature icon below. Text automatically adapts for contrast.”
```

```
国家自然奇观微缩立体模型

创建一个等距视角的微型3D立体模型，突出展示来自[国家名称/星球]的自然奇观[NATURAL WONDER]。  
采用40–45°俯视角度，材质使用基于物理渲染（PBR）的有机写实质感。光线柔和且富有氛围感。  
抬高的底座展现地形层次、植被及周边地理环境。添加风格化的小型探险者或野生动物，无需面部细节。  
背景为纯色[BACKGROUND COLOR]。  
顶部中央以粗体显示[国家名称]，第二行显示[NATURAL WONDER]，下方配以简约自然图标。文字自动调整以确保与背景形成对比。
```

<img src="../..//images/2026-02-05/2019342167102353672_1.jpg" alt="影棚中的超写实时尚肖像">

```
Defined From Every Angle.

Prompt: 

{
  "subject": {
    "identity_reference": "uploaded face and body reference",
    "identity_accuracy": "100% same facial structure, hair, skin tone, body shape, outfit, accessories; do not alter identity",
    "pose_variations": [
      {
        "name": "front-facing medium shot",
        "description": "upper to mid-body, facing camera directly, elegant and confident posture"
      },
      {
        "name": "front-facing extreme close-up",
        "description": "focus on face and eyes, capturing expression and makeup details"
      },
      {
        "name": "low-angle full-body shot",
        "description": "camera slightly below, looking upward, powerful stance emphasizing outfit and height"
      },
      {
        "name": "high-angle shot",
        "description": "slightly above, looking down, soft editorial perspective"
      },
      {
        "name": "side profile portrait",
        "description": "exact profile of face and hair, elegant posture, studio lighting highlights contours"
      },
      {
        "name": "34 profile (three-quarter turn)",
        "description": "torso and face turned three-quarters, emphasizing silhouette and outfit details"
      },
      {
        "name": "over-the-shoulder view",
        "description": "subject looks slightly away from camera, shoulder closest to camera in foreground"
      },
      {
        "name": "Dutch tilt",
        "description": "slightly rotated camera angle for dynamic editorial feel, maintaining consistent pose"
      },
      {
        "name": "mid-walk candid",
        "description": "natural motion captured, subtle step forward, relaxed arms, editorial energy"
      }
    ]
  },
  "wardrobe": {
    "description": "maintain exact outfit, fabric, and accessories as in identity reference",
    "consistency": "all panels must match perfectly"
  },
  "environment": {
    "setting": "studio environment",
    "background": "clean, minimal, neutral or light-grey cyclorama",
    "lighting": "soft professional studio lighting, subtle fill, flattering highlights"
  },
  "camera": {
    "shot_type": "varies per panel as specified",
    "angle": "specified per panel (low, high, front, profile, Dutch tilt, mid-motion)",
    "depth_of_field": "shallow, subject in sharp focus, blurred background"
  },
  "composition": {
    "grid": "3x3 contact sheet",
    "spacing": "consistent, balanced layout",
    "framing": "each panel fully showcases pose and outfit while keeping identity clear"
  },
  "aesthetic": {
    "style": "editorial fashion photography",
    "mood": "cinematic, professional, high-end",
    "color_palette": "maintain wardrobe and skin tones as reference, neutral background"
  },
  "render_quality": {
    "realism": "ultra-photorealistic",
    "resolution": "high (8K recommended)",
    "detail_focus": "fabric texture, skin, hair, accessories, lighting consistency across panels"
  },
  "negative_prompts": [
    "identity change",
    "distorted anatomy",
    "extra limbs",
    "blurred face",
    "motion artifacts",
    "low resolution",
    "inconsistent outfit or hair",
    "harsh shadows",
    "plastic skin",
    "busy background"
  ]
}
```

```
从每个角度精准呈现。

提示词：

使用上传的人脸与身体参考图作为身份基准，确保面部结构、发型、肤色、体型、服装及配饰100%一致，不得更改身份特征。  
包含以下多种姿态变化：  
- 正面中景：上半身至中身，正对镜头，姿态优雅自信；  
- 正面特写：聚焦面部与双眼，清晰呈现表情与妆容细节；  
- 低角度全身照：镜头略低于视线，仰拍展现强大气场，突出服装与身高；  
- 高角度镜头：略高于主体俯拍，呈现柔和的时尚编辑视角；  
- 侧面肖像：精确侧脸与发型轮廓，姿态优雅，影棚灯光强调面部线条；  
- 3/4侧脸（四分之三转体）：身体与面部呈四分之三角度，突出剪影与服装细节；  
- 越肩视角：主体略微侧头避开镜头，靠近镜头一侧肩膀位于前景；  
- 倾斜镜头（Dutch tilt）：相机轻微旋转，营造动态时尚感，同时保持姿势一致；  
- 行走抓拍：自然行走瞬间，微微迈步，手臂放松，散发时尚活力。

服装需完全复刻参考图中的款式、面料与配饰，所有画面必须严格一致。  
场景为影棚环境，背景干净简约，采用中性或浅灰色无缝背景布。  
灯光为柔和专业影棚光，辅以微妙补光，呈现讨喜高光。

各画面镜头类型依上述姿态分别设定，角度包括低角度、高角度、正面、侧脸、倾斜及动态中景，景深较浅，主体清晰锐利，背景虚化。

构图采用3×3接触印相式排版，间距均衡统一，每格完整展现姿态与服装，同时确保身份清晰可辨。

整体风格为时尚杂志摄影，氛围电影感、专业且高端，色彩忠实还原参考图中的服装与肤色，背景保持中性。

渲染质量要求超写实，建议8K高分辨率，重点呈现面料质感、肌肤、发丝、配饰细节，并确保各画面灯光一致性。

避免以下内容：身份改变、解剖结构扭曲、多余肢体、面部模糊、运动伪影、低分辨率、服装或发型不一致、强烈阴影、塑料感皮肤、杂乱背景。
```

## <a id="">手机屏幕里的冬日青年接住现实汽水</a> (<a href="https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2019057751260090731" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Smiling Khan</a>)

<img src="../..//images/2026-02-04/2019057751260090731_0.jpg" alt="手机屏幕里的冬日青年接住现实汽水">

<img src="../..//images/2026-02-04/2019057751260090731_1.jpg" alt="手机屏幕里的冬日青年接住现实汽水">

```
A hyper-realistic surreal photograph of a modern smartphone lying flat on a wooden table, its screen acting as a portal between two worlds. From inside the phone screen, a smiling young man dressed in winter clothing—puffer jacket, knit beanie, gloves—extends his hands outward into the real world, holding a clear glass. From outside the phone, a real human hand pours a bright neon-green carbonated drink into the glass, with liquid splashes frozen mid-air as it crosses the boundary between the digital screen and reality. Snowflakes drift inside the phone screen while the real environment remains warm and minimal. Ultra-detailed textures, cinematic lighting, shallow depth of field, photorealistic surrealism, high-resolution editorial photography.
```

```
一张超写实的超现实主义摄影作品：一部现代智能手机平放在木桌上，其屏幕化作连接两个世界的门户。从手机屏幕内部，一位身穿冬季服装（羽绒夹克、针织毛线帽和手套）的微笑青年将双手伸向现实世界，手中握着一只透明玻璃杯；而来自屏幕外的一只真实人类的手正将明亮的霓虹绿色碳酸饮料倒入杯中，液体飞溅的瞬间被定格在跨越数字屏幕与现实边界的空中。雪花在手机屏幕内缓缓飘落，而现实环境则温暖而极简。画面呈现极致细腻的质感、电影级光影、浅景深效果，融合写实与超现实风格，具有高分辨率的杂志级摄影水准。
```

```
A high-resolution, hyper-realistic portrait of a smiling young woman with curly dark hair, wearing a stylish black wool coat and a textured grey knit scarf. She is standing still and looking directly at the camera with a confident expression. The background is a busy New York City street with people and a yellow taxi blurred by heavy motion, creating a cinematic 'frozen in time' effect. Soft, natural daylight, 8k resolution, shot on 35mm lens, deep depth of field on the subject, sharp facial details."
​Tips for a Better Result:
​Aspect Ratio: If your tool supports it, use a 9:16 ratio to get that vertical, tall look.
​Lighting: Mentioning "Golden Hour" can add a warmer, more glowy feel to her skin if you want to change the mood.
​Style: Adding "Shot on Fujifilm" or "Kodak Portra 400" often helps AI generate more realistic skin tones and less "plastic" textures.
```

```
一张高分辨率、超写实的年轻女性肖像，她有着深色卷发，面带微笑，身穿时尚的黑色羊毛大衣和有纹理的灰色针织围巾。她静止站立，自信地直视镜头。背景是繁忙的纽约街头，行人和一辆黄色出租车因强烈的动态模糊而虚化，营造出电影般的“时间凝固”效果。柔和的自然日光，8K 分辨率，使用 35mm 镜头拍摄，人物景深深，面部细节锐利清晰。
```
